# Proof Debt Ledger

Auto-generated by `(proof-debt-ledger)`.  3145 proven theorem(s); each is certified by the kernel relative
to the `asserted` facts listed as its outstanding base.

## Reading an entry

A proof's **bill** is the set of `asserted` facts it ultimately
leans on -- resolved transitively: a cited *proven* lemma
contributes its own bill, a *primitive* axiom or *definitional*
fact contributes nothing, and an *asserted* fact contributes
itself.  Each such fact is a **leaf** of the bill.

- **`proven modulo 0`** (an *empty bill*) -- the proof rests only
  on the trusted base (the fixed primitive axioms, the kernel
  inference rules, and definitions).  Nothing asserted stands
  between it and the kernel; this is the strongest thing a proof
  can report, and it carries **no** trust tier.
- **`proven modulo {a, b, ...}`** -- the proof is unconditional
  *given* a, b, ...  Discharging every leaf (proving it, or making
  it a definition) would collapse the bill to 0.

The **trust tier** `[trust: K]` of a non-empty bill is the warrant
of its **weakest** leaf -- one shaky leaf caps the whole proof.
Tiers, weakest to strongest (from `*warrant-kinds*`; see the
manual, ch. Classification):

| tier | the weakest leaf is... |
|------|------------------------|
| **`none`** | asserted with **no warrant at all** -- a backlog item, not a category |
| `hand-wave` | a heuristic / plausibility argument, not rigorous |
| `well-known` | a standard textbook fact, asserted without argument |
| `reference` | cites a specific named source |
| `informal` | a rigorous paper-proof exists, just not mechanised in VNB |
| `proof` | a machine-checked VNB proof exists |

So `[trust: none]` is the **weakest** report (some leaf justifies
nothing), and `modulo 0` -- no tier -- is the strongest.
